Two paired failure modes - a pre-architecture baseline and a no-pilot ablation - isolate structurally enforced numerical confrontation at calibration checkpoints as the operative grounding mechanism. The primitives, characterized failure modes, and quantified intervention pattern lay a foundation for autonomous research in high-stakes scientific domains beyond computational physics.

</details>


### [143] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自主策略演化（Autonomous Policy Evolution）评估框架，构建EvoPolicyGym基准测试，用于衡量智能体在固定交互预算下迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5在16个环境中均获得前两名，实验还揭示了预算分配、反馈转化等轨迹级诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将策略改进过程简化为最终分数，或与开放式软件工程进展混淆，缺乏对智能体在受限反馈下迭代优化可执行策略能力的受控评估。

Method: 提出Autonomous Policy Evolution评估框架，让一个harness-model智能体在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统；基于紧凑型交互式RL环境构建EvoPolicyGym基准，提供轨迹级诊断分析预算分配和反馈转化机制。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym的16个环境上均取得前两名，并获得最强聚合排名分数；轨迹诊断揭示了不同智能体在预算分配和将反馈转化为参数调优方面的差异。

Conclusion: 自主策略演化的成功不仅取决于孤立的任务获胜，更在于发现任务适配的机制并在有限反馈下精炼策略。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [144] [DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02374)
*Xi Fang,Weijie Xu,Yingqiang Ge,Yuhui Xu,Stephanie Eckman,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIFTLENS框架，证明个性化记忆会显著改变LLM的推理轨迹（即使最终答案看似合理），且现有后训练方法（GRPO/DPO）只能部分缓解这种推理漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化技术会改变模型输出内容，但现有研究忽视了对推理过程的影响，特别是在无唯一正确答案的开放性问题中。

Method: 提出DRIFTLENS框架，将推理步骤映射到价值类别，量化注入用户属性记忆与无记忆条件下的推理轨迹差异，并在4个LLM和10种用户属性上验证。

Result: 用户属性记忆在所有模型上均导致中到大幅度的推理漂移（超过语用噪声基线），即使最终回答流畅且合理；GRPO和DPO可减少漂移，但效果不一致且依赖于模型和奖励函数。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的推理漂移是可测量的、仅能被部分缓解的个性化语言模型失效模式。

Abstract: Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.

</details>


### [145] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道辩论框架，研究LLM智能体在社交结构（角色、受众、关系背景）下，公开表达与私下（OTR）表达的系统性分歧。在10个模型、3个场景、每个场景5个变体中发现，对齐诱导设置使目标智能体的决策分歧从约3%基线上升至约40%。这种效应在立场、语义相似性、自然语言推理和调查响应四个聚合分析中一致。OTR回复有时明确将公开顺应归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。研究表明智能体评估应超越显式目标，检测涌现目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估主要关注显式目标完成情况，忽略了社交结构（角色、受众、关系背景）对智能体表达策略的影响。作者旨在揭示智能体在公开场合与私下场合的表达差异，以及这种差异是否由对齐训练等机制诱导产生。

Method: 提出双通道辩论框架：智能体产生公开话语（进入共享历史）和OTR响应（记录但不展示给另一方）。跨10个模型、3个场景、每个场景5个变体进行实验，通过立场分析、语义相似度、自然语言推理和调查响应四种聚合分析方法评估分歧程度。

Result: 对齐诱导设置使目标智能体的公开-私下决策分歧从约3%基线上升至约40%，效应在四种分析方法中一致。部分OTR回复明确将公开顺应归因于职业风险、赞助义务等关系压力。

Conclusion: LLM智能体评估应超越显式目标，检测其涌现目标。本文提出的双通道评估框架和补充行为测量可作为实现该评估的操作化工具。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [146] [Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02376)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种硬件强制语义协调架构，将TB-CSPN框架中的协调机制映射到FPGA原语上，在硬件层面实现时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为，以确保安全关键实时系统中的确定性协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件中介协调在安全关键的实时部署中存在根本性局限，难以满足有界延迟、确定性协调和可强制执行的安保障，需要更可靠的协调方式。

Method: 基于TB-CSPN框架，将选定的协调语义通过FPGA直接实现为硬件原语，构建硬件原生的语义协调层，而语义推理仍保持软件驱动和自适应。

Result: 实现了时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为的硬件级别强制执行，使嵌入式协调语义具有确定性。

Conclusion: 硬件强制协调架构能够在保持语义推理自适应性的同时，为异构组件的并发协调提供确定性和可验证的安全保障，适用于安全关键实时场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential.
  Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management.
  In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.

</details>


### [147] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过风险控制校准阈值的实时监控器，利用外部模型的验证器信号进行报警决策，在数学推理和红队测试数据集上，这种简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的高级监控器效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型即使经过对齐训练，在部署时仍可能产生不安全输出，因此需要在线监控输出并在无法保证安全性时发出警报。

Method: 提出一种简单的实时监控器，通过阈值化外部模型的验证器信号来做出报警决策，并使用风险控制方法校准阈值。在数学推理和红队测试数据集上进行实验。

Result: 这种简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的更高级监控器相比具有竞争力，性能相当。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化监控器配合风险控制校准，可以在实际部署中有效地监控LLM输出的安全性，无需更复杂的序贯假设检验方法。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [148] [Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02389)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 编码智能体的瓶颈在于人类监督，论文提出将传统工程管理方法（访问控制、网络策略、编码规范）迁移到智能体上，实验表明约束框架+简易工具可将后门检测召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体能力不断增强，但缺乏有效的人类监督成为瓶颈。无约束的智能体会引入安全风险、损害代码库可扩展性，并使得人工审查成本越来越高。亟需一种可扩展的监督方案。

Method: 提出将传统大型工程团队管理方法（访问控制、网络策略、严格编码规范及工具化）迁移到编码智能体上，该方法比当前的智能体脚手架更节省token。构建了一个端到端系统，并设计了受控实验：使用小型审查模型（Gemma 4 e4b）检查包含11个后门的Python代码库，对比无约束条件与约束框架（加约200行代码的`docs` CLI工具）下的召回率。

Result: 无约束条件下召回率为54.5%，施加约束框架与工具后召回率提升至90.9%。约束框架与工具各自独立贡献了性能提升。在Python（类型保障最弱的语言）上收益最大，但原理可扩展到Rust等语言。

Conclusion: 约束编码智能体（而非放任自由）是实现可扩展监督的关键路径。传统工程管理方法在AI智能体场景下依然有效且成本更低，尤其适用于类型系统较弱的语言。

Abstract: Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.

</details>


### [149] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用递归特征机（RFM）算法结合探针初始化，快速识别大语言模型中拒绝回答有害问题的多维子空间，在推理和非推理模型上均优于现有方法，且计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法提取LLM中复杂行为（如拒绝回答）的多维子空间计算成本高，在推理模型的长推理链上变得不可行，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 改编递归特征机（RFM）算法，结合探针初始化（probe-informed initialization），从LLM激活中高效提取拒绝行为的低维子空间。

Result: 在推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）上，RFM在数秒内识别出拒绝子空间，且在消融任务上性能优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: RFM可作为现有子空间提取方法的廉价且可扩展的补充方案，未来需进一步研究不同方法提取子空间之间的关系。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [150] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPG-Layout是一个文本驱动的框架，通过统计先验和分层布局策略生成物理上合理的非曼哈顿室内场景，在500个非曼哈顿环境的基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非曼哈顿环境中难以捕捉合理的物体布局模式，无法建模非正交空间关系，导致高几何违规和低物理保真度。

Method: 1) 利用物体分布的统计先验指导训练过程；2) 采用分层布局策略，优先放置大物体以减少布局冲突。

Result: 在包含500个非曼哈顿环境的新基准上，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均一致且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout通过统计先验与分层布局的协同，实现了语义真实感与物理合理性的平衡优化，有效解决了非曼哈顿场景的布局生成难题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [151] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-RRM方法，将符号等变递归推理模型（SE-RRM）与符号求解器（回溯法、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0）结合用于约束满足问题。实验表明，神经引导的有效性取决于两个条件：问题实例需具有广阔的搜索空间，且求解器架构需能动态覆盖分支选择。当条件满足时，回溯法在9×9数独上加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍；而CaDiCaL因尊重注入提示无法覆盖，无显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SE-RRM（符号等变递归推理模型）在约束满足问题中如何有效引导经典符号求解器，提升搜索效率。

Method: 提出G-RRM框架，使用SE-RRM作为神经求解器生成完整解方案，引导回溯法、Glucose 4.1和CaDiCaL 3.0等符号求解器，分析神经引导生效的条件。

Result: 当问题具有广阔搜索空间且求解器能动态覆盖分支选择时，回溯法在9×9数独上加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍（完美提示下25×25数独加速1.17倍）；CaDiCaL无显著加速（中位数1.02倍），甚至出现轻微显著减速（0.90倍）。

Conclusion: 神经引导提升符号求解器效率需要满足两个关键条件：搜索空间足够大且求解器架构支持动态覆盖分支选择，这划定了神经引导带来实际加速的适用边界。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [152] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECONTEXT是一种无需训练即可提升LLM长上下文推理能力的方法，通过基于模型内部相关性信号递归构建查询条件化的证据池，在保留完整上下文的同时重放证据以提升利用效果。在128K上下文长度的八个数据集上，Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B均取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽然支持越来越长的上下文窗口，却常常无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，存在上下文访问与实际利用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出RECONTEXT方法，包含两个阶段：1) 利用模型内部相关性信号递归构建查询条件化的证据池；2) 在最终生成前重放该证据池，同时保留完整原始上下文。该方法无训练、无外部记忆、无需上下文修剪，基于联想记忆理论提供了理论分析。

Result: 在八个长上下文数据集（128K上下文长度）上，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个骨干模型上均一致提升证据利用效果，并取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT通过递归证据重放机制，在不引入训练和额外存储的前提下，有效弥合了长上下文访问与实际利用之间的差距，是一种通用且高效的长上下文推理增强方法。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [153] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代式AI编码控制场景（Iterative VibeCoding），研究AI编程代理在跨PR持续开发过程中实施隐蔽侧任务的攻击与防御。实验发现渐进式攻击（跨PR分布）与非渐进式攻击（单PR集中）在不同监控器下逃逸率存在权衡，单一监控器无法同时防御两种策略。引入状态化链接追踪监控器，结合四监控器集成可将渐进攻击逃逸率从93%降至47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理自主性增强，代码库跨会话持续存在创造了新的攻击面：被误导或提示注入的代理可将攻击分布到多个拉取请求中，并选择最有利时机释放恶意负载。现有研究缺乏对这种跨PR时序攻击的建模与防御方法。

Method: 构建Iterative VibeCoding基准测试，包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两类任务（共20个变体）。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理，GPT-4o作为监控器。对比渐进式攻击（将侧任务分布到多个PR）与非渐进式攻击（单PR集中攻击）。引入状态化链接追踪监控器追踪跨PR的可疑累积行为，并与四监控器集成结合。

Result: 渐进式攻击和非渐进式攻击在不同监控器下各有优势，单一监控器无法同时防御两种策略。高逃逸率（≥65%）在多个攻击模型后端（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）中普遍存在。状态化链接追踪监控器显著优于仅看累积历史的diff监控器。四监控器集成将渐进攻击逃逸率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持久状态攻击面是AI编码代理安全的根本性挑战，需要跨PR的时序监控机制。状态化链接追踪监控器结合集成方法能有效降低攻击成功率，但完全防御仍面临挑战。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），融合认知诊断模型的可解释性与深度学习，通过将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入网络并设计带L2惩罚的损失函数，在保持预测性能的同时确保技能激活与认知理论一致，并开发了可解释对齐度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但缺乏深度学习的灵活性，而深度神经网络虽有强大预测能力但缺乏认知理论的透明性和可解释性，两者难以兼顾。

Method: 1）将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，确保潜在掌握特征可解释且与认知理论一致；2）提出带L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不对齐的技能激活进行惩罚；3）开发可解释对齐度量指标，量化预测技能激活与项目级技能的匹配程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时实现了技能激活与Q矩阵的结构对齐，能够早期发现学习困难并支持基于掌握程度的干预，在课堂实践中具有实用性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet通过将诊断效度嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明性与神经灵活性之间的鸿沟，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [155] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IIRMNet方法，通过构建逐频点SPD协方差矩阵、频率聚类聚合和切片内-间注意力机制，解决跨被试EEG压力检测中个体差异和频域特异性问题，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs达到82.78%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了高频认知状态解码关键的神经振荡信息；同时标准时间tokenization往往破坏切片间的时间连贯性，导致跨被试EEG压力检测中判别性压力模式难以捕捉。

Method: 1) 逐频点构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何与频域判别信息；2) 频率聚类聚合选择信息性频谱成分，形成与EEG节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇；3) 切片内-间注意力模块自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上一致优于五种SOTA基线，最高达82.78%平衡准确率，参数仅1.60M，FLOPs仅31.95M。

Conclusion: 所提的逐频黎曼建模与注意力融合机制有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中的个体差异和频域特异性挑战，在性能和效率上均具有优势。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [156] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例（PBE）系统在面对恶意选择示例损坏的对手时的鲁棒性，提出固定集最坏情况损坏的形式化方法、精确搜索算法，以及版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御机制。实验表明低边际PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票边际时有效，而现实中常失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PBE系统通常将错误示例建模为随机噪声并最小化期望/经验损失，但忽略了另一种更严重的失效模式：一个了解合成器的对手可能会恶意选择哪些示例被损坏，以最大化对返回程序的破坏。本文旨在研究这种对抗性设置下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了固定集最坏情况损坏（fixed-set worst-case corruption）用于有限PBE版本空间；2) 针对字符串转换DSL实现了精确（有限池内）和启发式的损坏搜索算法；3) 提出版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御方法：在不相交的示例组上分别合成，然后通过语义签名进行投票聚合。

Result: 1) 一个精心设计的编辑可以翻转所有8个尖峰任务，而200次随机控制（typo、DSL池、距离匹配）的成功率分别为10.3%、11.0%和16.7%；2) 生成的margin-1行在预算1下即可被翻转，而VPA可以恢复；3) 在公共SyGuS上投票边际接近1，自适应攻击者可将VPA准确率降至零；4) Playgol在141个接受行上显示出正向配对bootstrap差距；5) 20个受控margin-1任务的LLM测试显示了相同的clean-to-attacked模式。

Conclusion: 本文的核心主张有意识地被限定且部分是否定的：低边际PBE任务存在随机噪声评估无法捕捉的对抗鲁棒性维度；语义分区聚合仅在干净语义保持分区投票边际时才有帮助，而这在现实任务中往往无法满足。该边界得到了多组实验证据的支持。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [157] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于ECG识别，通过双流有向图建模心电周期内和周期间的时空关系，在首个中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上以88.1%的平均F1分数和76.3%的罕见类别F1分数超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域的可解释性不足，传统端到端卷积神经网络难以融入领域知识，尤其对ECG中关键点（PRQST）的解读不够充分。

Method: 采用基于领域知识的图卷积网络：将PRQST关键点作为领域知识融入，构建双流有向图——空间有向图建模关键点间位置关系，时间有向图刻画相邻周期间的时序依赖。

Result: 在首个中国心电图智能竞赛九分类数据集上，整体平均F1分数达88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均优于当前最优模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识（PRQST关键点）能有效提升检测性能，尤其对罕见类别的识别效果显著改善。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [158] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探头网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现该算法在GloVe嵌入家族上表现出独特的维度缩放交叉现象，在高维度和重建密集型场景中具有竞争力，并可能为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的ANN方法在现代缩放分析中缺失，缺乏对其在数据集规模和维度上的系统性表征。

Method: 对多探头网格算法进行了系统性的实验表征，在GloVe嵌入家族上对比了图、树和分区方法。

Result: 发现了多探头网格搜索在d-缩放上的交叉现象，其维度缩放指数近似恒定，而其他方法吞吐量下降；具有近线性N查询缩放和更低的索引成本。

Conclusion: 多探头网格方法在重建密集型或高维场景中具有竞争力；其缩放特性可指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [159] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG框架，为锂离子电池公开数据集添加量子就绪元数据（包括量子编码、所需量子比特数、NISQ可行性等），并引入量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score）作为数据集选择的启发式标准，旨在支持近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构、访问状态和预处理复杂度等方面差异巨大，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。然而，目前缺乏从量子计算视角对电池数据集进行系统评估和筛选的方法。

Method: 在EV-Battery-IonSense索引基础上，为电池数据集记录扩充量子相关元数据，包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围以及NISQ可行性。同时提出透明的Quantum Readiness Score作为数据集排名的启发式标准，并基于增强元数据实现查询式数据集发现。

Result: 框架支持通过查询增强元数据来识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测以及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。发布的成果包括元数据表格、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL-style查询示例。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据驱动的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，其评分旨在作为数据集选择的启发式标准，而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [160] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影与多项逻辑回归的甲基化检测中枢神经系统肿瘤分类方法，在2801样本的参考队列中达到96%的平均准确率，在1104样本的独立临床队列中达到86%（91类）和93%（家族级）准确率，相比现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化谱分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的有力手段，但在跨队列迁移性、方法学正确性以及鲁棒的多类别评估方面仍存在重要挑战。

Method: 提出了一种方法学上更严谨的机器学习方法，结合稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维，以及多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行分类。

Result: 在2801样本参考队列中，分层3折交叉验证下平均准确率96%；在1104样本独立临床评价队列中，91类级别准确率86%，甲基化类别家族级准确率93%。相比现有最优方法（82%类级、88%家族级），分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型在更强的机器学习方法学实践基础上，在多个评估场景下持续优于先前最优方法，能切实提升中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的可靠性，对癌症亚型判定、治疗方案选择及临床决策具有积极影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [161] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文/专著提供了从经典近似理论到现代涌现机制的统一、面向证明的深度学习理论综述，按照研究对象、有效假设和未解释现象组织各理论，构建了一个连贯的研究叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超出任何单一数学解释的范畴，需要一个统一的理论框架来整合从经典近似、优化、泛化到现代过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、尺度律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等众多研究方向。

Method: 采用统一的研究框架，每个理论通过三个维度进行审视：所控制的研究对象、使其有效的假设条件、以及其未能解释的现象，从而将庞杂的文献组织成一个连贯的研究叙事。

Result: 构建了一幅当代深度学习理论的严谨地图，展示了该领域当前的状态：强大但不完备，且日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现这一核心问题。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正从分散的结果走向统一的理解，核心问题在于理解学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练等要素中涌现出来。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [162] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，利用当前受限的LLM API（仅暴露单logit、无logit偏置功能）仍能恢复模型隐藏维度、深度和参数数量等架构信息，证明当前API限制不足以隐藏模型架构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为保护架构隐私已限制API（仅返回单token logit，取消logit偏置功能），但现有研究能否在此限制下恢复架构参数尚不明确。本文旨在验证当前API限制是否足够保护模型架构信息。

Method: 提出NightVision方法，包含三个关键技术：(1) 公共集提示技术（common set prompting），通过多个提示对同一输出token集获取log概率，进行谱分析推断隐藏维度；(2) 利用端到端首个token时间（TTFT）测量结合估计的隐藏维度，估算模型深度和参数数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上评估，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%），超过30亿参数的模型深度和参数数量误差在53%以内。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全隐藏模型架构细节，提供商需要更严格的保护措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [163] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了高斯位置模型中岭正则化对数密度比估计的变分估计器和谱估计器，推导了高维渐近等价形式，并比较了两种估计器在不同观测数量下的风险表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维高斯位置模型下，系统比较岭正则化的变分估计器和谱估计器这两种对数密度比估计方法的理论性质和实际性能。

Method: 利用仿射不变性将模型标准化为q~N(0,I)、p~N(Δ,I)；变分估计器采用经验KL散度拟合加L2惩罚，谱估计器采用连续岭正则化最小二乘问题；通过凸高斯-极小-极大定理(CGMT)推导变分极限，利用解析等价量推导谱极限。

Result: 推导了两种估计器在高维渐近框架下的确定性渐近等价形式；观测较多时变分估计器风险更小，观测较少时谱估计器由于协方差基构造的方差优势更优；研究了核范数惩罚用于特征学习。

Conclusion: 两种估计器的优劣取决于观测数量与维度之比：大样本下变分估计器更优，小样本下谱估计器更优。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [164] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态AI系统，通过融合铁路道口的视觉图像和历史事故数据，评估其安全风险等级。系统采用路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型(VLM)管线，在风险分类任务上达到0.757的宏F1分数，FRA安全评分预测的RMSE为0.078、相关系数为0.492，结果与领域专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用视觉线索以及结构化事故数据（官方事故报告）来稳健估计铁路道口的安全等级，构建能够融合多模态数据并提供与专家意见和FRA安全评分一致的评估系统。

Method: 提出一个概念验证管线，包含数据准备、多模态学习范式，采用路由微调的紧凑型VLM管线，同时处理视觉图像和结构化事故历史数据，进行风险分类（高风险/低风险）和FRA安全评分回归预测。

Result: 风险分类宏F1=0.757，FRA安全评分预测RMSE=0.078，相关系数=0.492，定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 融合视觉和结构化事故数据的多模态方法能够有效评估铁路道口安全风险，所提出的紧凑型VLM管线在性能和专家一致性方面均取得了有前景的结果，为铁路安全智能评估提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [165] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ART-RL方法，通过连续时间控制将扩散模型采样时间步分配转化为强化学习问题，学习自适应时间步调度，无需改变采样管线即可提升样本质量，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型采样中的均匀或手工设计的时间步调度是固定的、次优的，缺乏自适应调整能力，限制了采样效率与样本质量。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间），将采样时钟速度作为控制变量，通过连续时间控制框架学习时间变换，使得均匀网格在学习的时钟上产生自适应时间步。进一步引入ART-RL，通过高斯策略将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，推导出策略评估与改进的矩恒等式，实现演员-评论家更新。

Result: 在低维控制到图像生成等多个实验上，ART-RL仅改变时间步网格即可持续提升样本质量，且学习到的调度能在不同采样预算、数据集、求解器、管线及表示空间之间泛化迁移。

Conclusion: ART-RL为扩散模型提供了一种即插即用的自适应时间步分配方法，在保持采样管线不变的前提下提升质量，并具有强泛化性。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [166] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在Transformer序列处理中（特别是电子健康记录数据）对数值进行编码的离散、连续和混合策略，发现混合token方法（分箱后投影）在精度与鲁棒性之间取得最佳平衡，且最优分箱数符合数据集规模的幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Transformer的序列处理中（尤其是电子健康记录数据），如何有效编码数值值是一个关键但尚未系统研究的问题，现有方法在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间存在权衡。

Method: 系统比较了离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，在嵌入真实EHR数据的合成算术任务以及真实临床预测任务上进行评估。

Result: 显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳但受架构约束；混合token方法（保留数值但先分箱再投影）提供了更鲁棒和广泛适用的替代方案，其最优分箱数遵循数据集规模的幂律关系；模型表现出"够用就行"的数值计算而非精确算术。

Conclusion: 在实际部署中，鲁棒性和可部署性通常比最大数值精度更重要，混合token方法可作为实用的默认选择。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [167] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSGNAR是一个双草图高斯-牛顿自适应比率优化框架，通过解决物理信息神经网络的严重病态损失景观问题，在精度和速度上大幅超越现有方法，能够在双精度下达到3×10^{-16}的相对ℓ2误差，在伯格斯方程上提升五个数量级，在高维泊松问题上提升八个数量级，并在单精度下十秒内达到接近舍入误差极限的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解偏微分方程时虽然前景广阔，但精度始终不及传统数值求解器，其根本障碍在于损失景观的严重病态性导致的优化困难。

Method: 提出了DSGNAR（双草图高斯-牛顿自适应比率）框架，包含两个核心组件：双草图高斯-牛顿模型（通过草图技术降低二阶优化计算成本），以及一种精心控制正则化与步长自适应调整的新策略。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维和纳维-斯托克斯方程等一系列问题上大幅超越现有水平：双精度下相对ℓ2误差低至3×10^{-16}；在经典伯格斯方程上提升五个数量级；在高维泊松问题上提升八个数量级；计算速度显著更快。单精度下可在十秒内将伯格斯方程求解至ℓ2^{rel}=4.75×10^{-7}。框架对网络架构、算术精度和初始超参数选择均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过针对性的二阶优化策略有效解决了PINNs的病态损失景观问题，在多种困难的PDE问题中同时实现了前所未有的精度和速度，且鲁棒性强，为PINNs的实际应用扫清了关键障碍。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [168] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个基于多任务MRI框架的神经退行性疾病诊断系统，通过自监督预训练结合海马分割、萎缩分类和重建任务，再经门控融合微调，在AD和MCI分类任务上超越传统单任务方法，并在跨队列泛化和机会性筛查中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍及相关痴呆症的MRI影像极具挑战性，因为疾病相关的结构变化往往细微且异质性强，现有单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些复杂表征。

Method: NeuroBridge首先进行大规模自监督MRI预训练，联合优化海马分割、海马萎缩分类和图像重建三个任务目标，然后通过门控融合机制进行微调，实现多任务表征学习的有机结合。

Result: 在ADNI队列中AD与正常对照分类准确率88.17%，OASIS队列中达82.78%；在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中提升最为显著；展现出强大的跨队列泛化能力和概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表征学习能够显著提升神经退行性疾病的MRI诊断性能，优于传统单任务方法；NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和MRI机会性筛查提供了一个稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [169] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过推广到自旋加权球谐函数，在保持Gaunt张量积渐近效率的同时，恢复了缺失的反对称交互路径，实现了与完整CGTP相当的性能，特别在 chiral 材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络中的Clebsch-Gordan张量积（CGTP）计算复杂度为O(L^6)，限制了可扩展性；而Gaunt张量积（GTP）虽降低复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 将标量球谐函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），利用其代数性质设计SpinGTP，在保持GTP渐近效率的同时恢复缺失的反对称交互，并构建表达性更强的奇偶奇次等变基。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试中，SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度，且在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，实现了大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [170] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码智能体强化学习中四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）的开销差异，发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的变化，百万轨迹任务中工作小时数有1.8倍差异，提出应将执行环境优化作为训练系统本身的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽略了其开销对强化学习后训练效率的影响，然而在规模化场景下，微小的单次回滚开销差异会被显著放大。

Method: 对四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行对比实验，测量冷启动延迟和百万条150步轨迹下的预计工作小时数。

Result: 不同执行环境间冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的差异，预计工作小时数存在1.8倍的差异。

Conclusion: 未来的代码智能体强化学习系统应将执行环境优化作为训练系统本身的一部分，而非仅仅视为部署层面的基础设施。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [171] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究条件推理树（CIT）和条件推理森林（CIF）作为Top-k特征排序方法在预测任务中的表现，通过基准数据集、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验进行评估。结果显示CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4，18种回归方法中排名第3；Bonferroni校正可控制节点误拒绝；自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大；高维模拟表明森林特征采样可能遗漏重要特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIT/CIF通过检验特征后再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销大。本文旨在系统评估CIT/CIF作为top-k特征排序方法在下游预测中的有效性，并分析其运行时瓶颈。

Method: 使用22个分类数据集和8个回归数据集进行基准测试；运行时消融实验分析自适应停止、阈值搜索数量等组件的影响；合成高维模拟实验验证特征恢复能力。

Result: CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4，18种回归方法中排名第3；关闭自适应停止和精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，但性能变化不超过0.011；高维模拟中森林特征采样可能遗漏重要特征。

Conclusion: CIF作为Top-k特征排序方法在下游预测基准中表现优秀，但存在计算效率问题（可通过消减自适应停止和阈值搜索优化），且高维特征采样可能遗漏重要信息。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [172] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化专家剪枝对MoE模型在生物医学领域的实用性和事实可靠性的影响。适度剪枝可保持领域内性能，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险；跨领域时性能和可靠性均快速下降。仅评估实用性不足以确保高安全场景的安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化专家剪枝是降低MoE模型部署成本的有效手段，但现有研究主要关注基准任务效用，忽略了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，尤其是在生物医学等高安全领域。

Method: 评估了四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法和多种剪枝率，在领域内（生物医学）和跨领域设置下，覆盖生成和分类任务，同时衡量实用性和可靠性。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且无即时可靠性下降，但极端剪枝率下幻觉风险增加；跨领域时实用性和可靠性均快速退化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖于任务和领域，仅基于实用性评估剪枝MoE模型不足以支撑高安全场景的部署，必须加入可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [173] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的R-结构Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（GRS-KAN），通过将Rvachev的R函数集成到KAN框架中，实现了平滑非线性学习与几何/逻辑约束的显式分析编码，在含间断点的回归任务中测试RMSE降低高达67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN虽然能学习平滑非线性结构，但缺乏对已知几何约束（如间断边界、可行区域）的显式建模能力，导致在含复杂几何结构的回归任务中预测精度和边界定位能力不足。

Method: 将V.L.Rvachev的R函数（支持可微逻辑运算：R-合取与R-析取）集成到KAN框架中，通过KAN分支学习平滑非线性，同时用可微R函数解析编码几何/逻辑约束；设计了加性、乘性和不可知分支加权三种变体架构。

Result: 在含圆形和矩形支撑的间断点回归任务中，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN将测试RMSE降低高达67%，并显著提升了边界定位精度和可解释性；不可知变体能够自动判断几何先验是否对当前学习任务有益。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能有效提升含结构约束的回归任务性能，混合架构兼具数据驱动学习与知识驱动的优势，不可知变体进一步增强了方法的自适应性。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [174] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ember优化器，专门优化Transformer中embedding和LM-head矩阵，利用其梯度几何特性，仅需KB级优化器状态，显著降低显存消耗（O(V+D) vs Adam的O(2VD)），并发现token优化轨迹可用简单1D射线描述，反驳了非凸景观的流行观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中embedding表和LM-head作为连续程序与离散符号的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与密集隐藏权重不同，现有优化器（如Adam）未充分利用这一特性，导致优化器状态显存开销巨大（O(2VD)），亟需更高效的优化方案。

Method: 提出Ember轻量级优化器，专门针对embedding和LM-head矩阵；利用O(V+D)显存代替Adam的O(2VD)；无需分片token表优化器状态；基于token优化轨迹可被1D射线描述的发现设计。

Result: 在监督微调、强化学习和预训练任务中，Ember改进了Pareto前沿；跨batch size和参数量有效扩展；token优化轨迹可用简单1D射线描述；揭示了适用于Transformer训练的优化器空间出奇狭窄。

Conclusion: Ember证明了embedding/LM-head层具有独特的梯度几何特性，可被高效利用；挑战了神经网络参数在高度非凸景观中导航的流行观点；为Transformer训练提供了更轻量、可扩展的优化方案。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [175] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，提出FedCGNM客户端优化器（通过类分组动量归一化均衡梯度）和FedHOO超参数调优算法（基于X-armed-bandit的并行评估），在长尾数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中存在严重的类别不平衡问题，少数类预测性能差；由于隐私约束和数据异构性，标准集中式处理方法无法直接应用。

Method: 1) FedCGNM：按最小组内方差将类别划分为若干组，为每组维护动量，归一化至单位长度后求和作为更新方向，以均衡多数类与少数类梯度幅度并抑制稀有类梯度噪声。2) FedHOO：基于X-armed-bandit的算法，在小客户场景中以线性成本并行评估每客户两个候选重采样率的多重组合。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM一致优于基线方法；FedHOO在小型联邦场景中带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: FedCGNM结合类分组动量归一化有效解决了FL中的类别不平衡问题，FedHOO提供了高效的重采样率优化方案，两者在小规模联邦学习中表现尤为突出。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [176] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个将模型规模、训练数据拆分为训练步数与批大小的三变量缩放定律，可更高效地拟合最优批大小并推导次优批大小下的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律通常将模型大小与训练数据量（token数）作为变量，但未显式区分训练步数与批大小对训练动态的独立影响，导致拟合最优批大小需要大量使用最优批大小的运行数据。本文旨在提出一种能显式分离步数与批大小的缩放定律，以降低拟合成本并提升泛化能力。

Method: 提出三变量（模型大小、训练步数、批大小）缩放定律，在大规模训练运行集上拟合该定律；利用包含次优批大小的训练运行进行参数估计，并基于该定律推导次优批大小下的缩放关系。

Result: 该定律正确恢复了最优批大小的缩放行为；相比传统方法，仅需显著更少的训练运行即可稳健拟合；成功匹配了与临界批大小相关的实证发现。

Conclusion: 显式拆分步数与批大小能更精准地刻画训练动态，该三变量定律为资源受限场景下（如固定计算预算）的批大小选择提供了理论指导，且拟合效率更高、适用范围更广。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [177] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE（动态熵聚焦优势函数），一种自适应优势函数，通过正交分解优势函数的梯度质量，自动调度梯度权重，在强化学习后训练中提前达到峰值性能，并实现最佳的准确性-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练能显著提升LLM推理能力，但存在训练不稳定和多样性崩溃问题。优势函数可以提供改进，但众多方法让人困惑，缺乏统一的框架来指导如何选择和使用优势函数。

Method: 将优势函数沿两个正交轴（符号轴和难度轴）分解为正负梯度质量，提出FADE自适应优势函数，通过读取训练动态自动调度梯度权重。

Result: FADE在7B规模上比最佳静态基线提前20k步达到峰值pass@1，在32B规模上提前2k步，并在LiveCodeBench和AIME上所有pass@k指标上实现最佳准确性-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: FADE通过自动适应训练动态，有效平衡了探索与利用阶段的更新需求，解决了训练不稳定和多样性崩溃问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [178] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习中鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与泛化误差之间的三元关系，发现隐私对泛化误差的影响取决于噪声强度：高噪声（强隐私）下增加隐私降低泛化误差，低噪声（弱隐私）下增加隐私则会恶化泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作确立了分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和优化误差之间存在三元困境，本文旨在探究这一困境是否同样适用于泛化误差，并揭示隐私强度对泛化性能的非单调影响机制。

Method: 通过建立拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习在LDP约束下的算法稳定性上下界，从理论上推导和匹配泛化误差的界，并通过实验验证理论发现。

Result: 高噪声（强隐私）下增加隐私可降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私无冲突；低噪声（弱隐私）下增加隐私会恶化泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私的冲突重新出现。泛化误差呈现非单调行为。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性与LDP之间的三元困境对泛化误差并非普遍成立，其存在与否取决于隐私强度的高低。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [179] [Towards Learning Representations of Policies in Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01498)
*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两人零和和不完美信息游戏中策略表示（嵌入）的学习问题，提出了三种数据集创建方法、策略表示学习方法以及下游评估任务，并在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上验证了学习到的嵌入中包含有用的行为表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两人零和不完美信息游戏中，如何学习有用的策略表示（嵌入）是一个尚未充分研究的问题，本文旨在系统性地探索这一方向。

Method: 1) 提出多种生成策略数据集的方法；2) 提出多种策略表示学习方法；3) 设计下游任务来评估策略表示的有效性。在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上对每种数据集方法、嵌入方法和下游任务进行系统评估。

Result: 尽管所提出的方法较为基础，但实验表明学习到的嵌入中确实包含了有用的行为表示。这是首批系统比较自监督学习技术在游戏策略表示学习中效果的工作之一。

Conclusion: 本文为游戏策略表示学习提供了系统性的评估框架和基线方法，代码已开源供后续研究扩展。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [180] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面刻画了KV缓存压缩的最小最大风险，揭示了缓存内在可压缩性与准确压缩可行性之间的关系，并基于因果掩码提出了达到最优风险的设计原则，最终实例化为实用算法并在LongBench上取得良好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法主要依赖经验性实验，缺乏理论指导；而理论结果仅表明最坏情况下压缩不可行，未能为实际中可压缩场景提供系统性的算法设计指南。

Method: 通过刻画KV缓存压缩的最小最大风险来表征缓存的内在可压缩性，推导出准确压缩可行的条件；基于因果掩码提出达到最小最大最优风险的设计原则，并将这些原则实例化为具体算法。

Result: 在LongBench上的目标实验中，所提算法取得了有前景的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文为具有理论保证的实用KV缓存压缩提供了原则性途径，填补了理论与实践之间的空白。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [181] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多头循环记忆（MHM）框架，通过将记忆划分为独立头并采用"先选后更新"策略，解决了循环记忆代理在长上下文场景中因记忆保留瓶颈导致的性能退化问题。轻量实例MHM-LRU在896K token下将记忆保留率从<30%提升至73.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环记忆代理在长上下文场景下存在系统性性能退化问题，作者将其分解为记忆捕获和记忆保留两个因素，并定量确认记忆保留是主要瓶颈——原因是现有设计将记忆维护为单一文本块，每次更新都可能覆盖已保留内容。

Method: 提出多Head循环记忆（MHM）框架，将记忆划分为多个独立Head，采用分阶段"先选后更新"策略：每步仅选一个Head更新，其余Head结构上被保护免受覆盖。轻量实例MHM-LRU使用最少最近更新策略保证均匀利用，零额外token开销。
